How we support static egress IPs: The recommended approach is to use a VPN connection with Tailscale and route your sandbox traffic through an exit node you control with a known static IP. From your firewall's perspective, all sandbox traffic will originate from that single IP — giving you full control over what to allowlist. This works out of the box in Daytona since Tailscale falls back to TCP when UDP isn't available. Full guide here: daytona.io/docs/en/vpn-connections#…"}
